5b1(mdo sde): pa dang //brtson 'grus bsam gtan mig yo blo gros rnon po rnams//nyin bde'i ltos med shin tu yun ring yang

6a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /skyabs med g.yo zhing phongs pa du mas yobs khyab la//thugs rje chen pos thugs ni spro ba bskyed 'gyur te/

6a4(mdo sde): kyis mnyan par gyis//de nas dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' bskal pa mang por bsod nams
6a5(mdo sde): dang ye shes kyi tshogs dpag tu med pa du ma nye bar bsags pas//byang chub kyi phyogs yongasu rdzogs par mdzad pa//nyon 
6a6(mdo sde): mongs pa'i chu bo stobs dang ldan pa shin du chod pa rnams la thugs rjes yongs su bskul ba//gzhan gyi don mdzad pa'i phyir

6b4(mdo sde): bzod par byas pa dang /rtag tu lhod pa med pa'i spro ba dang /'bras bu dang /bcas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba la ma chags pa dang/
6b5(mdo sde): yod pa med pa'i don la rnam par dpyod pa dang/ldan pas gzhan gyi don la brtson ba'i blo gros gyis//shin tu mi bzad pa'i sdug bsngal 
6b6(mdo sde): gyi tshor ba rnam pa mang pos sems la gdungs pa'i sdug 'phongs pa'i srog chags rnams la mchog tu snying rje skyed nas/byams pa'i stobs 
6b7(mdo sde): dang zla 'od dang/shi bi dang/glang po dang thams cad sgrol dang/bzod par smra ba dang/spre'u yi bdag po dang/migs bzangs dang/sman 
7a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /chen po la sogs pa skyes pa'i rab stong phrag snyed du/྾ e ma'o kye ma bdag gis 'jig rten long ba 'dren pa med pa/ I

7a7(mdo sde): ser snas kun du 'chings pa/legs par spyod pa la 'jug pa la rgyab kyis phyogs shing nyes par spyod pa la kun du gzhol bar gyur pa rnams la/
7a8(mdo sde): bdag sangs rgyas su gyur nas/sems can ma da(྾)gral ba rnams ni bsgral bar bya'o//ma grol ba rnams ni grol bar bya'o//dbugs ma phyin pa 
7a9(mdo sde): rnams ni dbugs dbyungs bar bya'o//yongs su mya ngan las ma 'das pa rnams ni yongs su mya ngan las zla bar bya'o//zhes ngas yun ring 
7a10(mdo sde): du mngon par 'dod pa yin na/da ni don de bya ba'i dus la bab pa yin gyis/ma la bdag gis 'jig rten gyis gnas skabs ci zhig yin pa ltas na/
7b1(mdo sde): gdul bar nus so//gdul bar nus so//zhes de ltar rnam par dgongs nas/'jig rten 'di dag thams cad la gzigs pa 'jug

7b4(mdo sde): su ni ngan song la 'bab/su ni ngan song las bton te/mtho ris dang thar ba'i 'bras bu la gzhag par bya/su ni 'dod pa'i 'dam na bying ba la lag 
7b5(mdo sde): nas dran par bya/su ni 'phags pa'i nor dang bral pa rnams la 'phags pa'i nor gyi dbang phyug gi bdag po la bzhag par bya/gang gis dge 
7b6(mdo sde): ba'i rtsa ba ma bskyed pa dag ni bskyed par bya/gang gi dge ba'i rtsa ba ma bskyed pa dang ni yongs su smin par bya/gang gis yongs su rmin pa rnams ni rnam 
7b7(mdo sde): par grol bar bya/zhes ye shes kyi gzigs pa 'jug pa mdzad pa na/srog chags brgya stong dpag tu med pa la la ni/dge ba'i rtsa ba ma bskyed 
7b8(mdo sde): pa bskyed par 'ongs pa dang /la la dge ba'i rtsa ba ma smin pa yongs su smin par nus pa dang /la la dge pa'i rtsa ba yongs su smin pa bde 'gror 'gro 
8a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /ba la gzhol ba dang //la la 'bras bu dang po thob par gzhol ba dang //la lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang //phyir 
8a2(mdo sde): mi 'ong ba dang //dgra bcom pa nyid dang //rang byang chub 'thob par gzhol ba dang //la la bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa byang chub pa'i bskal pa 
8a3(mdo sde): dang ldan pa dang //la la ni sngon gyi smon lam rnams nye bar bsags pas rkyen las rag las pa gang zhig ma thogs pas dam pa'i chos las yongs su nyams par 
8a4(mdo sde): gyur pa gzigs so//gzigs nas kyang 'di snyam du dgongs te//kye ma'o bdag gi yun ring po nas yang dag par rtog pa rdzogs par gyur cig/nga'i 
8a5(mdo sde): ngal ba 'bras bu dang bcas par gyur to//zhes dges shing //ngas bya ba dang ni byas so//ngas khur bskur ba rka ba ni/mthar phyin te//gang nga'i yongs su dag 
8a6(mdo sde): pa'i rgyud dang ldan pa'i zhing du gyur cing 'dul bya'i skye'o rnams yun ring por mi thogs par pad ma'i tshal yongs su smin cing kha bye la ma reg pa'i nyi ma shar bas rgyas par 'gyur 
8a7(mdo sde): ba lta bur gyur gyis phyi shol mi bya'o////'dir gnas bzhin du yang thams cad rdzogs par bya bar nus pa ma yin pas na/bzhes pa'i dus ni ma yin gyis/
8a8(mdo sde): ma la bdag mi'i 'jig rten du song la der phyin nas brtson 'grus gyi grogs dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i bla na med pa'i ye shes rtogs par bya ste/'di 
8b1(mdo sde): rnams bskal pa ji lta ba bzhin du 'di rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed cing yongs su smin la rnam par grol ba bya zhing /skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi 
8b2(mdo sde): ba dang /mya ngan dang /smre bsngag 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi bde ba'i sdug bsngal gyi rgya mtshor lhung ba/skyabs med pa/yongs su skyob pa 
8b3(mdo sde): med pa srog chags rnams//dam pa'i chos bstan pa'i lag pa brkyang pas/gnod par bya'o//thog ma med pa'i dus nas rjes su 'brel ba'i 'dod chags 
8b4(mdo sde): dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug gi me kun gdungs pa rnams la//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i chu'i rgyun blugs pas sdug bsngal ma lus pa bsal 
8b5(mdo sde): bar bya'o//zhes de lta bu'i thugs la dgongs pa nyes pa bskyed nas/'dzam ba'i gling du 'jug pa mdzad pa na/rigs dang /yul dang dus dang /brgyud dang /
8b6(mdo sde): bud med ste//gzigs pa rnams pa lngas gzigs par mdzad do//byang chub sems dpa' rnams rgyu gang gis rigs la gzigs par mdzad ce na/byang chub sems 
8b7(mdo sde): dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na/bzhugs pa ni 'di snyam (du )dgongs te/byang chub sems dpa' rnamasarigs ji lta zhig tu nying mtshams sbyor ba len snyam na/bram ze'i 
8b8(mdo sde): rigs sam/rgyal rigs su yin na/de la res 'ga' ni bram ze'i rigs btsun par bkur/res 'ga' (na)rgyal rigs te/da lta ni rgyal rigs btsun par bskur bar 
9a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /gzigs nas/ma la bdag la gzhan dag byang chub sems dpa' rigs ngan par nyid mtshams sbyor ba slangaso//zhes bzhi 'dis kha zer 
9a2(mdo sde): bar mi 'gyur/bdag gis rgyal rigs su nyid mtshams sbyor ba blang bar bya'o snyam mo//'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' thogs ma med pa'i dus nas smad par mi 'gyur/
9a3(mdo sde): ba'i las byas shing bsags pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos bshad pa 'bras bu med pa yin pas/rgyu 'dis na byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
9a4(mdo sde): rigs la gzigs par mdzad do//byang chub sems dpa' rnamasargyu gang gis yul la gzigs par mdzad ce na/byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhug+sa 
9a5(mdo sde): pa ni 'di snyam du dgongs te/byang chub sems dpa' rnamasayul ji lta ba zhig tu nying mtshams sbyor ba len snyam na/'bras dang /bu ram shing dang /ba lang 
9a6(mdo sde): dang /ma he phun sum tshogs pa/slongs mo pa brgya 'du ba mtha' khob kyi skye bo med pa dbus pa'i skye bo gnas pa gang du skye bo gcig btul bas/skye bo'i tshogs 
9a7(mdo sde): chen po 'dul bar 'gyur ba'i yul gang yin par gzigs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du dgongs te /dbus kyi yul 'di ni bu ram shing gi phreng dang /
9a8(mdo sde): 'bras dang /ba lang dang /ma he phun sum tsho gas pa//slongs mo pa brgya 'du ba//mtha' khob kyi skye bo med pa/dbus pa'i skye bo'i gnas pa gang du skye bo gcagi 
9b1(mdo sde): btul bas skye bo'i (tshogs)chen po btul bar 'gyur pa yin gyis/ma la bdag la gzhan dag byang chub sems dpas yul mtha' khob rnams su nyid mtshams/
9b2(mdo sde): sbyor ba blangs so zhes zer ba 'dis kha zer bar mi 'gyur bar bdag gis yul dbus su nyid mtshams sbyor ba blang bar bya'o snyam mo//'di ltar byang chub 
9b3(mdo sde): sems dpa' thogs ma med pa'i dus nas/smad par mi 'gyur ba'i las byas shing bsags pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos bshad pa 
9b4(mdo sde): 'bras bu med pa ma yin pas rgyu 'di ni/byang chub sems dpa' yul la gzigs pa mdzad do//byang chub sems dpa' rnams rgyu gang gis na/dus la gzigs par 
9b5(mdo sde): mdzad ce na/byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs pa ni 'di snyam du dgongs te/byang chub sems dpa' rnamasadus ji lta ba zhig tu mdzam bu'i gling du nyid 
9b6(mdo sde): mtshams sbyor ba len snyam na/skye dgu'i tshe lo yar skye ba na/brgyad khri thub pa yan chad nyid mtshams sbyor ba mi len la/mi rnams kyi tshe lo mar 'grib brgya thub pa man 
9b7(mdo sde): chad nyid mtshams sbyor ba me len no//byang chub sems dpa'-i rgyu gang gis rnams kyi tshe lo brgyad khri thub thub pa yan chad nyid mtshangs sbyor ba len par mi mdzad ce na/
9b8(mdo sde): mi rnams kyi tshe lo brgyad khrir thub na/yid 'byung bar rka zhing skyo bar bya ba dka' la/blun zhing dbang po brtul ba dang /bag med cing bde ba mang bas/bden pa rnams 
10a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /kyi snod du mi 'gyur ba'i phyir ro//mar 'grib pa lo brgya man chad du yang snyigs ma lnga po 'di lta ste/tshe'i snyigs ma dang /nyon mongs pa'i 
10a2(mdo sde): snyigs ma dang /sems can gyi snyigs ma dang /lta ba'i snyigs ma dang /dus kyi snyig ma shas che ba yin pas bdag la gzhan dag byang chub sems dpas snyig+sa ma 
10a3(mdo sde): lnga shas che ba'i dus su nying mtshams sbyor ba blangs so zhes gzhi 'dis kha zer bar mi 'gyur ro//'di ltar byang chub sems dpas thog ma med pa'i dus nas/smad 
10a4(mdo sde): par mi 'gyur ba'i las byas shing bstsags pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos bshad pa 'bras bu med pa ma yin pas/rgyu 'dis ni byang chub 
10a5(mdo sde): sems dpa' rnams dus la gzigs pa yin no//byang chub sems dpa' rnams rgyu gang gis rgyu la gzigs par mdzad ces na/byang chub sems dpa' dga' 
10a6(mdo sde): ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs pa ni 'di snyam du dgongs te/byang chub sems dpa' rnamasargyud ci 'dra ba zhig tu nying mtshams sbyor ba len snyam na/rigs gang zhig btsan 
10a7(mdo sde): par bkur zhing ma myes zung bdun pa myes gyi bar spyod pa la skyon med par gzigs nas/de('di) snyom   du dgongs te/shakya'i rigs rgyud 'di ni btsun par bkur ba 
10a8(mdo sde): yin zhing mang pos bkur ba nas bzung ba ste/spyod pa skyon med pa yin gyis ma la bdag la gzhan dag byang chub sems dpas rgyud ngan pa las nying mtshams 
10b1(mdo sde): sbyor ba blangs so zhes gzhi 'dis kha zer bar mi 'gyur bar/bdag gi rgyal po zas gtsang ma rgyud du nyid mtshams sbyor ba blang ngo snyam mo//'di ltar byang chub sems 
10b2(mdo sde): thog ma med pa'i dus nas sman par mi 'gyur ba'i las byas shing bsags pa dang //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos bshad pa 'bras bu med pa ma yin pa(sa)//
10b3(mdo sde): rgyu 'di na byang chub sems dpa' rgyud la gzigs par mdzad do//byang chub sems dpa' rnams rgyu gang gis bud med la gzigs par mdzad ce na/byang chub  
10b4(mdo sde): sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs pa ni 'di snyam du dgongs te/byang chub sems dpa' rnamasabud med ci 'dra ba zhig gis rum du nying mtshams sbyor ba  
10b5(mdo sde): len snyam na/de 'di snyam du dgongs te/bud med gang zhig gzugs bzang mo dang ldan pa/rigs dang ldan pa/rigs 'phel bar 'gyur ba/khro bag 
10b6(mdo sde): can ma yin pa/phra ma can ma yin pa/ngag rtsub pa ma yin pa/dad pa dang ldan pa dang /rang bzhin bzang ba/bsam pa dge ba/rgyud phun sum tshogs pa/
10b7(mdo sde): rigs mtho ba//nye du'i rigs dang ldan pa//shes rab phun sum tshogs pa//gtong ba phun sum tshogs pa//mtshan nyid mi g.yo ba/rang bzhin brtan la rgod 
10b8(mdo sde): bag can ma yin pa/khyengs pa med pa/byams pa'i sems dang ldan pa/sdug cing yid du 'ong ba/'jig rten thams cad la chos dang ldan pa/bud med thams cad kyi 
11a1(mdo sde): mngon 'byung 
11a2(mdo sde): @#/ O /mchog tu gyur pa//sangs rgyas rnams la/e ma'o bdag sangs rgyas kyi yum du gyur cig ces smon lam btab pa dang /byang chub sems 
11a3(mdo sde): dpa' zla ba bcu rum du 'dzin nus pa dang /rang gi don du yang nyams par mi byed pa yin par gzigs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du dgongs te/sgyu 
11a4(mdo sde): 'phrul chen mo 'di ni ma myes dang pha myes zung bdun gyi bar du spyod pa la skyon med cing gzugs dang ldan pa dang /rig dang ldan pa dang /tshul dang ldan zhes bya ba nas/bud 
11a5(mdo sde): med thams cad kyi mchog tu gyur ces bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin te/khro bag can ma yin pa dang /rigs 'phel bar 'gyur ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' rum du zla ba bcur 
11a6(mdo sde): 'dzin nus pa dang /rang go don yang mi nyams pa yin la/'di ltar sgyu 'phrul chen mos sngon sangs rgyas rnams la kye ma bdag sangs rgyas kyi yumadu gyur cig ces smon lam
11a7(mdo sde): btab pa yang yin pas/bdag la gzhan dag byang chub sems dpa' mtshan ngan pa dang /ldan pa'i bud med kyi tsam du nying mtshams sbyor ba blangs so zhes kha zer bar mi 
11a8(mdo sde): 'gyuro//snyam mo 'di ltar byang chub sems dpas sman par mi 'gyur ba'i las byas shing bsags pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam+sa ni chos bshad pa 'bras bu med 
11a9(mdo sde): pa ma yin pas/rgyu 'dis na byang chub (sems)dpa' bud med rnams la gzigs par mdzad do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' gzigs pa lngas gzigs nas 
11b1(mdo sde): dga' ldan na spyod pa'i lha'i rigs rnams la gsungs pa/grogs po dag shes par gyis shig/nga 'dzam bu'i gling du babs nas yul dbus su nying mtshams 
11b2(mdo sde): sbyor ba len gyis khyed cag las gang dag bdud rtsi don du gnyer ba de phal cher yul dbus kyi grong khyer chen po drug po 'di lta ste/mnyan yod dang /sa ge 
11b3(mdo sde): ta dang /yangs pa can dang /tsam pa dang /rgyal po'i khab dang /ba ra na ser nying mtshams sbyor bar long shig/de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /dga' 
11b4(mdo sde): ldan gyi rig kyi lha rnams kyis byang chub sems dpa' la 'di skad ces gsol to//byang chub sems dpa' mkhyen par mdzod cig/da ltar 'jig rten ni sdig 
11b5(mdo sde): pas gos pa dang /skye dgu ni ma rungs pa'i bsam pa can/'dzam bu'i gling ni rtog ge drug dang /rjes su sgrogs pa drug dang /snyoms par 'jug pa drug gis 
11b6(mdo sde): bkrugs pa lagaso//de la rtog ge drug gang zhe na/'di lta ste 'od bsrung rdzogs byed dang /kun du rgyu gnag lhas kyi bu dang /smrang 'dod kyi bu mo'i bu yang dag rgyal ba 
11b7(mdo sde): can dang /mi 'pham skra'i la ba can dang /ka' t+ya'i bu nog can dang /gcer bu pa gnyen gyi bu'o//rjesu sgrog+sa pa drug gang zhe na/'di lta sta bram ze rgyus rgyal dang /bram ze rnam rdeg dang /
11b8(mdo sde): bram ze bzang ldan dang /bram ze tshang+sa pa'i tshe dang /bram ze tshad ma'i snying po dang /bram ze mar po'o//snyoms par 'jug pa gang zhe na/'di lta lte/rig byed kyi bu lhag spyod dang ///
11'a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /rgyu rtsal shes kyi bu ring pur dang /kun du rgyu rab bzang dang /bram ze'i khye'u kun rgyal dang /drang srong nyon mongs med dang /lteng rgyas 'od 
11'a2(mdo sde): bsrungs ralpa can no//'dir ni chos nyan pa'i slad du/byang chub sems dpa' khyed kyi gdan dpag tshad bcu gnyis bsham pa bgyi zhing /bdag cag 'di snyam du bdag cag 
11'a3(mdo sde): la dga' ldan gyi gnas na byang chub sems dpa' bzhugs pas chos bshad de/chos de bdag cag gis thos nas ci nas kyang bdag cag gis yun ring po'i don dang /sman pa dang /
11'a4(mdo sde): bde bar 'gyur ba de lta de ltar bsgrub par bgyid par sems legs so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /byang chub sems dga' ldan gyi lha rnams la 'di skad (ces)gsungs so//
11'a5(mdo sde): grogs po dag de'i phyir rol mo'i cha byed thams cad brdungs shig/dga' ldan gyi ris kyi lha rnams kyis rol mo'i cha byad thams cad brdungs ba dang /byang chub sems 
11'a6(mdo sde): dpas kyang dung bus te gsungs pa/grogs po dag 'di lhag par sgra gang che/bcom ldan 'das dung gi sgra lags so//grogs po dag ji ltar dung gi sgras rol mo'i 
11'a7(mdo sde): cha byad thams cad zil gyis gnan te/gnas pa de bzhin dungs kyang 'dzam bu'i gling du song la/rtog ge drug pa dang rjes su sgrogs pa drug dang /snyoms bar 'jug 
11'a8(mdo sde): pa drug zil gyis gnan nas/bdud rtsi rtogs par bya ste/bdud rtsis 'gro ba tshim par bya'o//mi rtag pa'i dung 'bud bar bya'o//stong pa nyid kyi rnga brdungs bar bya'o//
11'b1(mdo sde): zhes gsungs nas/tshigs su bcad de gsungs pa/seng ge gcig gis gcan zan tshogs ni du ma 'joms/rdo rje gcig gis brag gi rtse mo du ma 'jig/brgya 
11'b2(mdo sde): byin gcig gis lha ma yin du ma 'joms//nyi ma gcig gis mun pa du ma rnam par sel/grogs po dag khyed cig las gang bdud rtsi don du gnyer ba de/yul 
11'b3(mdo sde): dbus kyi grong khyer chen po drug tu nying mtshams sbyor ba long shig/de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin 'di snyam du sems te/byang chub sems dpa' 'di ni/
11'b4(mdo sde): btsun mo sgyu 'phrul chen mo'i lhums su nying mtshams sbyor ba len par gzhed kyis/ma la de la bdag gis gzi brjid sgrub par bya/lhums kyang sbyang bar bya'o snyam 
11'b5(mdo sde): nas/lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byani btsun mo sgyu 'phrul chen mo la gzi brjid srabs shing lhums kyang gtsang gtsang mar byas so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dga' 
11'b6(mdo sde): ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs pa/rigs dang /yul dang /dus dang /rgyud dang /bud med la gzigs pa ste/gzigs pa lngas gzigs pa nas/nam gyi gung la glang po chen bstan te 
11'b7(mdo sde): yum gyi lhums su zhugs so//gang gi tshe byang chub sems dpa' dga'i ldan gyi lha'i ris nas shi 'phos te/yum gyi lhums su zhugs pa de'i tshe/sa chen po 'di 
11'b8(mdo sde): shin du g.yos par gyur/'jig rten 'di thams cad snang ba chen pos gsal bar gyur cing /'jig rten ni 'jig rten gyis par gyi mun pa mun nag smag tu gyur pa/gang du 
12a1(mdo sde): mngon nyi ma dang zla ba 'di ltar rdzu 'phrul che ba/'di mthu che ba dag gis/'od kyis snang bar mi nus pa de dag kyang /de'i tshe snang ba chen pos 
12a2(mdo sde): gsal bar gyur to//der sems can gang dag skyes pa de dag rang gi lag pa brkyang pa mi mthong ba las/de rnams snang ba des gcig gis gcig mthong nas shes ldan dang sems 
12a3(mdo sde): can gzhan yang 'dir skyes so//shes ldan dag sems can gzhan yang 'dir skyes sa zhes bya bar shes so//ji ltar sprin ni rgya chen legs 'dus pa/chu mang bsags pa rlung gis 
12a4(mdo sde): bskyod pa ltar/de bzhin blo ldan de ni lhums su 'jug/ston ka'i sprin rum zla rgyas ji bzhin no//gang tshe rtsal ldan mnyam med nyer gshegs la/de tshe 'jig rten so so'i 
12a5(mdo sde): gling bar na/mun gyis khebs bsal phyogs kun shin tu ni/bsal snang byug gyur 'di 'dir chos nyid do//gang gi tshe byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan lha'i ris nas 
12a6(mdo sde): 'phos te/yum gyi lhums su zhugs par mdzad pa de nyid kyi mtshan mo sgyu 'phrul chen mos rmi lam bzhi mthong ste/glang po che'i phru gu dkar po zhig bdag gi rum g.yas pa 
12a7(mdo sde): dral nas zhugs pa dang /steng gi nam mkha' la skyil mo krung gis 'chag pa dang /seng ge'i bdan la 'dug nas skye bo'i tshogs chen po la chos ston pa dang /lha dang 
12a8(mdo sde): lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten bdag la phyag byed pa'o//rgyal po zas gtsang mas btsun mo las de thos pa dang /mtshan mkhan rnams la rmi lam 'di ltar ston 
12b1(mdo sde): pa yin dris pa dang /de rnams kyis smras pa/lha mkhyen par mdzod cig/gang btsun mo rmi lam du mthong ba glang po che'i phru gu dkar po zhig bdag gi rum g.yas/
12b2(mdo sde): pa dral nas zhugs pa zhes bya ba ni/'khor los sgyur ba'i sras bstam par 'gyur ro//steng gi nam mkha' la 'chag go/zhes bya ba ni rdzu 'phrul che ba dang /
12b3(mdo sde): mthu che bar 'gyur ro//seng ge'i gdan la bzhugs te chos ston to zhes bya ba ni//de yang seng ge'i bdan la bzhugs te 'khor bzhi la chos ston par 'gyur ro//
12b4(mdo sde): lha dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten bdag la phyag byed do zhes bya ba nas/gal te rab tu 'byung bar gyur na/lha dang lha ma yin du bcasapa'i 'jig rten gyis 
12b5(mdo sde): mchod cing brjod la mngon par bstod par 'gyur ro//chos nyid kyis gang gis tshe/byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi lha'i ris nas 'phos te yum gyi lhums na 
12b6(mdo sde): su zhugs pa de'i tshe/lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin gyis de la mi ma yin pa sus kyang mtshe bar mi 'gyur bar lha'i bu bzhi yum gyi srangs mar 'jog pa ni/lag na 
12b7(mdo sde): ral gri dang /lag na mdung dang /lag na mdung thung dang /lag na mda'o che'o//de la 'di skad ces bya ste/lha mchog bzhi la brgya byin gyis 
12b8(mdo sde): smras pa/khyed rnams song la lha mi rnams kyis ni/shin du mchog gyur yum de ltar su la yang /gnod mi byed par gus pas yongs su bsrung /de rnams 
13a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /shin du 'badabyas dga' ba dang /brgya byin lung ni sbyi bos gnan nas su/rnam kun mdu (thung)ral gri lag na thogs/byang chub 
13a2(mdo sde): sems dpa'i yum ni srung bar byed/g.yul du rgyal gyur lha bzhis sbas pa dang /de ni nyin mtshams sna tshogs rgyan cing zhing /lha mo rang khyim ngan nas rnam grol rol/
13a3(mdo sde): dga' ba'i (chal)chal (chal)na spyod pa lta bur spyod/de nas dus gzhan zhig na/btsun mo sgyu 'phrul chen mo 'di lta bu'i 'dod pa skyes te/kye ma'o lha brtson thams cad thar bar bgyi'o//
13a4(mdo sde): rgyal po zas gtsang mas kyang btsun mo'i tshig gnyan nas/brtson thams cad thar bar byas so//de nas dus gzhan zhig na btsun mo 'dod pa skyes pa/kye ma'o lhas 
13a5(mdo sde): grong khyer gyi shar phyogs kyi dbus su dman pa dang /long ba dang bkren pa dang /sdug 'phongs pa dang /slongs mo pa rnams la sbyin pa byin/bsod nams dag 
13a6(mdo sde): byas pa dang /lho dang nub dang /byang dang /sum mdo dbus su dman pa dang /long ba dang /bkren pa dang /sdug phongs pa dang /slongs mopa 
13a7(mdo sde): rnams la sbyin pa byin bsod nams ni bgyi'o//de nas rgyal po zas gtsang gyis btsun mo'i tshig mnyan nas grong khyer gyi shar phyogs dman pa dang /long ba dang /
13a8(mdo sde): bkren pa dang /sdug phos pa dang /slongs mo pa rnams la ji ltar mngon par 'dod pa dang /ji ltar slangs pa bzhin du sbyin pa byin bsod nams ni byas pa dang /
13b1(mdo sde): de bzhin du lho dang /nub dang /byang dang /sum mdo'i dbus su dman pa dang /long ba dang /bkren pa dang /sdug phongs pa dang /slongs mo pa rnams 
13b2(mdo sde): gang mngon par 'dod pa dang /ji ltar slangs pa bzhin ngas sbyin pa ni byin bsod nams ni byas so//gang gi tshe byang chub sems dpa' shAkya thub pa dga' ldan gyi lha'i ris nas 
13b3(mdo sde): 'phos nas/yum gyi lhums na bzhugs pa dang /mdzod kyi nang du rdzas gzhag pa bzhin du mngal gyi dri ma dang /sha ma'i dri ma dang /khrag gi dri ma dang /mi gtsang 
13b4(mdo sde): gang yang rung ba sna tshogs kyis ma spags so//dper na nor bu rin po che ka sha'i ras kyis mi go(sa) pa dang /ka shi ras kyang nor bu rin po ches mi gos pa de bzhin du/gang gi 
13b5(mdo sde): tshe byang chub sems dpa' yum gyi lhums su bzhugs pa na/mdzod kyi nang du rdzas bzhag pa bzhi du mngal gyi dri ma dang sha ma'i dri ma dang /khragagi dri ma dang /
13b6(mdo sde): mi gtsang ba gang yang rung ba sna tshogs kis ma spags so//ji ltar nor bu mtshon shel 'od gsal dang /ras gtsang ka sha'i ras kyang gos pa med/de bzhin thub chen 
13b7(mdo sde): lhums na bzhugs pa na/mngal gyi dri ma cis kyang gos pa med /gang gis tshe byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi lha'i rig nas 'phos nas yum gyi lhums na bzhug+sa 
13b8(mdo sde): pa de'i tshe/yum gyi lhums ni de thams cad yongs su rdzogs par bzhugs pa mthong ngo //dper na nor bu bai DUr+ya zur brgyad pa bzang po//rigs dang ldan pa/

14b1(mdo sde): te/ji srid mtsho'i bar du srog spyod pa spangs te/srog gcod pa las slar ldog go/ma sbyin par len pa dang /mi tshangs par spyod pa dang /brdzun du smra ba 
14b2(mdo sde): dang /'bru'i chang dang /sbyar ba'i chang myos par 'gyur ba bag med pa'i gnas spangs te/'bru'i chang dang /sbyang ba'i chang myos par 'gyurab bag med pa'i gnas

14b4(mdo sde): yum gyi chos nyid yin/chos nyid gang gi tshe/byang chub sems dpa' shAkya thub pa dga' ldan gyi gnas nas 'phos nas/yum gyi lhums na bzhugs pa de'i yum 'di lta ste/
14b5(mdo sde): skyes pa rnams la 'dod pa dang ldan pa'i yid gtod pa med do//nyon mongs pa rnams la chags par mi 'gyur zhing /de ni 'dod rgyas sems gdung mi 'gyur la/de yum 
14b6(mdo sde): 'dod pa'i yon tan ldan pa'i/skyes pa rnams la sems ni gtod pa med/de nas dus gzhan zhig na/btsun mo sgyu 'phrul chen mo 'dod ba skyes pa skye ma bdag gis 
14b7(mdo sde): rgya mtsho chen po bzhi'i chu btung ngo //snyam nas des rgyal po zas gtsang la smras pa dang /rgyal po zas gtsang gis mtshan mkhan rnams la smras pa/pir ci'i

17a2(mdo sde): chos nyid kyi gang gi tshe byang chub sems dpa' shAkya thub pa yum gyi lhums nas bltam pa dang /ral gri shubs nas phyung ba bzhin du mngal gyi dri ma dang /sha ma'i dri ma

23b3(mdo sde): na de nyid dang mtshan du dpag tshad cig kho ra khor yug tu gzigs so//las bcas las byung dri med spyan gyis ni/gzugs chen po dang de bzhin phra rab rnams/nyid dang 
23b4(mdo sde): dang mtshan du khor yug dpag tshad cig/mdun na gnas pa bzhin du 'ang gzigs par gyur/chos nyid kyi byang chub sems/dpa' bltam pa'i mod nas gsung snyan cing 
23b5(mdo sde): 'jebs pa dang /gsung snyan pa dang /gsung yid du 'ong ba yin te/dper na/gang can gyi bya skad snyan cing 'jebs pa dang /skad snyan pa dang /yid du 'ong ba de 
23b6(mdo sde): bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' bltam pa'i mod nas/gsung snyan cing 'jebs pa dang/gsung yid du 'ong ba yi no//ri 'o kun 'dzin la drang srong nyon mongs med ces 
23b7(mdo sde): bya ba mngon par shes pa lnga dang ldan pa byams pa'i bdag nyid can snying rje dang ldan pa/skye dgul mnyes shin ba zhig gnas te/de la tshe 'o mi sbyin zhes bya ba yod pa 
23b8(mdo sde): dang /de de'i tshe phug tu zhugs te bsams gtan byed pa na byang chub sems dpa' ba ltam ste/'jig rten thams cad snang ba rgya chen pos gsal bar gyur ba'i 
24a1(mdo sde): mngon @#/ O /snang ba de mthong ba dang /nyon mongs med la smras pa/mkhan po 'dir ni nyi ma thams cad dag/cig car byung bar 'gyur ram ci/'di ltar 
24a2(mdo sde): ri ni phug bcas pa/'di ni 'od gyis gsal bar gyur/nyon mongs med de smras pa/nyi ma'i 'od ni rgyun du rno//bu cig snang ba 'di dag ni/yan lag reg na 'od 
24a3(mdo sde): gsal bas/nges par thub pa de'i 'od/byang chub sems dpa' 'od chen ni/nges par lhums nas ltam gyur la/ltam nas sems dpa' chen po de'i/'od gzer gser 
24a4(mdo sde): dang 'dra ba ni/dri ma med pa 'di dag gis/'jig rten gsum ni snang byas yin/mes sbyin gyis smras pa mkhan po bzhud/ci re zhig 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por 
24a5(mdo sde): 'gyur ram/'on te bla na med pa'i ye shes rtogs par 'gyur ba rang nyid lta bar mdzod cig/drang srong nyon mongs med kyis smras pa/da lta ni rgyal po zas gtsang ma'i khyim/
24a6(mdo sde): nyi 'od kyi rgyal po dang /mkhar gyi rgyal po dang /tshong pa dang tshong dpon dang /ded dpon rnams dang /bram ze dang /khyim bdag brgya stong dpag tu med pas 
24a7(mdo sde): kun du khyab nas/'dug gis gang gis tshe de dben par gyur pa de'i tshe rgyal po zas gtsang ma'i gan du 'gro bar bya'o//de nas rgyal po zas gtsang ma 'di snyam du skye ma'o 
24a8(mdo sde): drang srong nyon mongs med rkad gis ser skye'i gzhir 'ongs na ci ma rung snyam du sems so//de nas drang srong nyon mongs med steng gi nam mkha' la mngon par 'phags 
24b1(mdo sde): nas rgyal po zas gtsang ma'i pho brang sgor phyin pa dang /ji tsam na sgo srungs kyis rgyal po zas gtsang ma la lha dgyes pa bskyed du gsol/drang srong nyon mongs /
24b2(mdo sde): med 'dir mchis so zhes rig par byas pa dang /rgyal po bzas gtsang mas thos te/dga' ba dang /mgu ba dang /rab tu dga' ba dang /mchog tu rangs 
24b3(mdo sde): pas byung nas mchog tu gus pas mchog de skad ces smras so//drang srong chen po legs par 'ongs so//dgos pa ci zhig la 'ongs/nyon mongs med kyis smras 
24b4(mdo sde): pa/rgyal po chen po bdag ni 'dir/khyed sras lta ba'i phyir mchis te/'jig rten gtso bo rnams 'dren pa/thub pa de ni mthong bar 'tshal/rgyal pos smras 
24b5(mdo sde): pa/gal te de lta na ni gzhon nu gnyid log gis/sang kyi bar du re shig sdod cig/nyon mongs med kyis smras pa/rigs dang ldan pa'i lha rnams ni/mtshan mo'i zhi 
24b6(mdo sde): cha nyal ba ltar/dgos pa don gnyer rnams kyi gnyid/mig la yun rings mi gnas so//de nas gzhon nu shAkya thub pa ma ma'i phang na 'dug pa drang srong nyon mongs 
24b7(mdo sde): med la byin pa dang /drang srong nyon mongs med kyis lag pa gnyis kyis blangs nas yud tsam rtag te smras pa/lha gzhon nu 'di mtshan mkhan rnams kyis ji skad lung 
24b8(mdo sde): stan/rgyal po zas gtsang mas smras pa/rin po che sna bdun dang ldan pa'i 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur ro zhes zer ro//de nas drang srong nyon mongs 
25a1(mdo sde): mngon @#/ O /med kyis byang chub sems dpa' mthong nas tshigs su bcad de smras pa/sa bdag rtogs pa po rnams blo 'khrul te/rtsod pa'i dus na 'khor 
25a2(mdo sde): lo la sgyur mi 'byung /bsod nams mchog gi chos kyi brjod 'di ni/nyes las rnam par rgyal nas sangs rgyas 'gyur/mtshan mi gsal dang ldan na mi dbang po//
25a3(mdo sde): gling bzhi rnams kyi bdag po dbang phyug 'gyur/thub dbang mtshan ni shin du gsal bas na/mi mchog sangs rgyas chos kyi 'khor lo sgyur/de nas drang srong nyon mongs 
25a4(mdo sde): med kyis bdag nyid tshe'i mtha'i nges pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' mngon par byang chub pa rtag pa la zhugs pa dang /byang chub sem dpa' lo nyi shu rtsa dgu bzhes 
25a5(mdo sde): nas khab nas byung nas/lo drug tu dka' ba spyod par 'gyur te/de nas bdud rtsi rtogs par 'gyur ba mthong ngo //bdag ni bar skabs der mchi ba'i dus byed 
25a6(mdo sde): par mthong nas/de mig mchi mas gang te 'dug pa rgyal po zas gtsang gis mthong nas/sngags nas tshigs su bcad de smras pa/skyes pa bud med rnams kyis 
25a7(mdo sde): mthong ba na/ngoms pa mi 'gyur dga' ba 'thob 'gyur ba/lus bzang dge byed de ni 'dir mthong nas/drang srong chen po ci'i slad du bzhin 'gyur/ga las bdag gi 
25a8(mdo sde): gzhon nu 'jigs pa 'am/tshe zad 'gyur ba nye bar mi gnas dang /de ni myur du legs par bdag la smros/khyod kyis mchi mas bdag ni dngang bar 'gyur//
25b1(mdo sde): drang srong nyon mongs med kyis tshigs su bcad de smras pa//gal te 'di'i steng Idu namakha' las//rdo rje ser drag 'phangs par bab gyur kyang //thub pa chen po'i sku 
25b2(mdo sde): la ba spu ni//nyag ma cig kyang g.yos par mi nus so//me chen rlung gi shugs kyis bdas pa dang //mtshon cha rnon po shin du mi zad dang //drag po'i dug dang sbrul 
25b3(mdo sde): gyi dug rnon yang //gzhon nu la bab skad cig 'jig mi 'gyur//thugs rje nyid las gnas gyur gang lags pa//'jigs pas nyen pa rnams la mi 'jigs stsal//
25b4(mdo sde): de ni 'jigs pa kun gyi 'jigs 'joms na//mi dbang 'di la 'jig pa ga la 'byung //'jig rten na ni bdag nyid che rnams kyi/gtsor gyur bdag nyid chen po gang 
25b5(mdo sde): zhig la//tshangs bcas lha yang rtag tu srung byed pa//mi dbang 'di la 'jigs pa ga la 'byung //mi dbang bdag ni rgud pa mthong nas ni//gang phyir zhi ba mi thob bdag 
25b6(mdo sde): tu mod//chos kyi mchog mdzod 'di(rang) phrad nas ni//don ma thob par bdag ni mchi bar 'gyur//gang dag smra ba dam pa mtshungs med pa//'di'i chos thos 'gyur zhing 
25b7(mdo sde): thob nas kyang //rab tu zhi ba'i pha rol 'gro de dag//skyid cing bsod nams ldan la rims nad bral//de nas drad srong nyon mongs med byang chub sems dpa' reg 
25b8(mdo sde): pa'i mthus de'i rdzu 'phrul nyams so//nyams pa dang nyon mongs med kyis smras pa//rkang pa kho na nas grong khyer nas phyir 'byung bar bya'o//de nas rgyal 
26a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /zas gtsang mas thos nas/grong khyer thams cad kyi rdo ba dang /gseg ma dang /gyo mo rnams bsal nas spos dang phye mas 
26a2(mdo sde): khyab par byed du bcug go/de nas drang srong nyon mongs med 'byor pa chen pos grong khyer nas byung ste rim gyis ri 'o kun 'dzin du phyin pa dang /mi sbyin la smras pa//ngas 
26a3(mdo sde): gzhon nu mthong ste/bla na med pa'i yes shes rtogs par 'gyur na/ngas ni de sleb par mi 'gyur te/nga ni bar skabs su dus byed par 'gyur gyis/khyod ni de'i 
26a4(mdo sde): thad du rab tu byung la/nga rgyal dang dregs pa nyes par bcom par 'gyur ba dang /tshangs par spyod pa la spyad par byas te/rigs kyis dregs par mi bya/rus kyis 
26a5(mdo sde): dregs par mi bya/rigs rgyud kyis dregs par mi bya ste/khyod kyis kyang yun ring po mi thogs par bdud rtsi 'thob par 'gyur gyis zhes smras nas/yang tshigs 
26a6(mdo sde): su bcad de smras pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tsho la 'dod na/shar phyogs su ni khyod 'dug na/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas drags ldan pa/rnyed bar dka' ba mthong bar 
26a7(mdo sde): 'gyur/de nas dus gzhan zhig nas/rgyal po zas gtsang mas gzhon nu ma ma'i phang na 'dug pa la yang dang yang du lta zhing nga'i bu ci 'dra ba de lta bu ni sa'i steng na su la 
26a8(mdo sde): yang med do//snyam du sems shing shin du dga' bar gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'khrungs nas ring po ma lon par ma ma'i phang du bzhugs nas gser 
26b1(mdo sde): gyi gzhong par 'bras dang sha bsregs pa'i chan gsol ba na/de ma mas bkab kyang ma thub par mang por gsol nas 'phrog par brtsams pa dang /'phrog 
26b2(mdo sde): ma nus nas des rgyal po zas gtsang la sbran pa dang /rgyal po btsun mo dang lhan cig gis 'phrog par brtsams pa des kyang ma nus so//de nas rgyal po blon 
26b3(mdo sde): po rnams ser skyan gnas pa'i skye bo'i tshogs rnams la bsgo na de rnams kyis kyang 'phrog par ma nus so//de nas glang po che lnga brgya la btags pa dang /byang chub 
26b4(mdo sde): sems dpas 'di rnams kyis bdag gi stobs nyams na mi rung gis/ma la bdag gis 'dzub mo bkug nas gser gyi gzhong pa nas bzung ngo snyam nas des 
26b5(mdo sde): 'dzub mo bkug pas gser gyi gzhong pa gzung nas drangs pa dang /glang po che lnga brgya po de dag kha phyir sgyur te drangs nas de dag gis kyang gser gyi gzhong 
26b6(mdo sde): pa 'phrog par ma nus so/ /rgyal po zas gtsang gis bsams pa ji ltar byang chub sems dpa'i lag pa g.yon pas glang po che lnga brgya drangs pa dang sbyar na/
26b7(mdo sde): gdon mi za bar lag pa gnyis kyis ni glang po che stong thub kyis de bas na gzhon nu'i ming glang po che stong gi stobs can zhes bdags so//grong khyer ser skya'i 
26b8(mdo sde): gzhi na gnod sbyin ni shAkya 'phel zhes bya ba zhig smyug mar gnas so//shAkya rnams kyis kun du spyod pa ni//shAkya su yang rung ba la khye'u 'am//bu mo 
27a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /btsas pa gang yin pa de gnod sbyin shAkya 'phel gyi rkad pa la phyag 'tshal du bzhugs pa'i phyir khrid pa yin pas/rgyal pos 
27a2(mdo sde): blon po rnams la bsgo ba/shes ldan dag deng la gzhon nu gnod sbyin shAkya 'phel kyi rkad pa la phyag 'tshal du chug shig/de nas byang chub sems dpa' glang po che la skyon 
27a3(mdo sde): te//dpung gi tshogs dpag tu med pas yongs su bskor te//grong khyer ser skya'i gzhir 'jug par brtsams pa dang //grong khyer ser skya'i bzhi sgra bcu gnyis dang bral 
27a4(mdo sde): bar gyur cig byang chub sems dpa' de nang du zhugs pa na thub pa bzhin du gnas par gyur to//de nas rgyal po zas gtsang mas bsams pa/grong khyer 'di lta ste 
27a5(mdo sde): glang po che'i sgra dang /rta'i sgra dang /shing rta'i sgra dang /dpung bu chung gi sgra dang /dung gi sgra dang /rnga bo che'i sgra dang /pe ta'i sgra dang /ra dung gi sgra dang /
27a6(mdo sde): grar gi sgra dang /glu'i sgra dang /rol mo'i sgra dang /sbyin pa ni byin/bsod nams ni gyis/bsnyen gnas la ni nye bar gnas/tshul khrims yang dag 
27a7(mdo sde): par blabs te gnas shing ces bya ba'i sgra ste/bcu gnyis dang bral zhing gzhon nu de nang du zhugs pa na thupa pa bzhin du zhi bar gnas pas/gzhon nu'i ming ni shAkya

27b1(mdo sde): gan du khyer ba dang /gnod sbyin shAkya 'phel gyis byang chub sems dpa' gnas dang nye bar phyin par mthong ngo //mthong nas kyang phyir byung nas/lus thams 
27b2(mdo sde): kyis byang chub sems dpa'i rkang pa gnyis la gtugs te phyag byas so//de mthong ba dang rgyal po zas gtsang mas smras pa/lhas kyang rkang pa gnyis la

27b4(mdo sde): de'i tshe rgyal po zas gtsang gis dgos pa'i ched la 'dug nas/don zab mo tshigs dang yi ge dag shes rab kyis so sor 'byed par byed do//de nas byang chub 
27b5(mdo sde): sems dpa' shAkya thub pa 'khor lnga brgya dang yi ge'i slob dpon srid bu go cha la yi ge slob par btad nas yi ge'i slob dpon yang dpan srin bu go bcas byang chub sems

28b1(mdo sde): du snun pa brlab par mi bya'o//de nas dus gzhan zhig na lha sbyin gyis ngang pa lcags mdas bsnun pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' steng gi khang bzangs kyi gzhi
